### [39] [DualGeo: A Dual-View Framework for Worldwide Image Geo-localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25533)
*Junchao Cui,Wenqi Shi,Shaoyong Du,Hang He,Xuanzi Ma,Hao Tang,Xiangyang Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DualGeo：融合图像-语义特征与双视角对比学习构建全局检索库，再通过地理聚类重排序+LMM精化，实现更鲁棒、更精确的全球化图像地理定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全球图像地理定位方法依赖对环境变化（如光照、季节、天气）敏感的视觉特征，且缺乏有效的后处理机制来过滤异常候选结果，导致定位精度受限。

Method: 提出DualGeo两阶段框架：（1）地理表征基础阶段——通过双向交叉注意力融合图像与语义分割特征，并采用双视角对比学习将融合特征与GPS坐标对齐，构建全局检索数据库；（2）地理认知精化阶段——利用地理聚类对检索候选进行重排序，再输入大型多模态模型（LMMs）进行最终坐标预测。

Result: 在IM2GPS、IM2GPS3k和YFCC4k数据集上，DualGeo超越现有最先进方法：街道级（<1 km）定位精度提升3.6%-16.58%，城市级（<25 km）定位精度提升1.29%-8.77%。

Conclusion: DualGeo通过特征融合与双视角对比学习增强地理表征，结合地理聚类重排序和大型多模态模型的精化推理，有效解决了环境鲁棒性和异常过滤问题，实现了更精确的全球化图像地理定位。

Abstract: Worldwide image geo-localization aims to infer the geographic location of an image captured anywhere on Earth, spanning street, city, regional, national, and continental scales. Existing methods rely on visual features that are sensitive to environmental variations (e.g., lighting, season, and weather) and lack effective post-processing to filter outlier candidates, limiting localization accuracy. To address these limitations, we propose DualGeo, a two-stage framework for worldwide image geo-localization. First, it establishes a geo-representational foundation by fusing image and semantic segmentation features via bidirectional cross-attention. The fused features are then aligned with GPS coordinates through dual-view contrastive learning to build a global retrieval database. Second, it performs geo-cognitive refinement by re-ranking retrieved candidates using geographic clustering. It then feeds them into large multimodal models (LMMs) for final coordinate prediction. Experiments on IM2GPS, IM2GPS3k, and YFCC4k show that DualGeo outperforms state-of-the-art methods, improving street-level (<1 km) and city-level (<25 km) localization accuracy by 3.6%-16.58% and 1.29%-8.77%, respectively. Our code and datasets are available : https://github.com/CJ310177/DualGeo.

</details>


### [40] [Control Your Queries: Heterogeneous Query Interaction for Camera-Radar Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25574)
*Jialong Wu,Yihan Wang,Matthias Rottmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了ConFusion，一种基于异构查询交互的新型相机-雷达3D目标检测器，通过异构查询混合(QMix)和交互式查询交换采样(QSwap)实现跨模态特征融合，在nuScenes数据集上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动驾驶中，相机-雷达融合能够提供互补的感知能力且部署成本较低。现有方法主要通过输入混合、特征图混合或基于查询的特征采样进行融合，但这些方法在查询初始化和跨模态交互方面存在不足，难以充分利用两种传感器的互补信息。

Method: 本文提出了一种新的融合范式——异构查询交互(Heterogeneous Query Interaction)，并设计了ConFusion检测器。具体包括：(1) 结合图像查询、雷达查询和可学习的3D世界查询以改善查询初始化和物体覆盖；(2) 异构查询混合(QMix)：在特征采样后执行专门的跨类型注意力机制，以整合互补的物体证据；(3) 交互式查询交换采样(QSwap)：通过注意力和几何约束允许相关查询交换信息丰富的特征令牌，从而改进特征采样。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，ConFusion达到了验证集59.1 mAP和65.6 NDS、测试集61.6 mAP和67.9 NDS的最先进性能。

Conclusion: 异构查询交互范式通过促进不同类型查询之间的深度交互，有效提升了相机-雷达融合3D目标检测的性能，证明了该方法在多模态感知中的优越性。

Abstract: In autonomous driving, camera-radar fusion offers complementary sensing and low deployment cost. Existing methods perform fusion through input mixing, feature map mixing, or query-based feature sampling. We propose a new fusion paradigm, termed heterogeneous query interaction, and present ConFusion, a camera-radar 3D object detector. ConFusion combines image queries, radar queries, and learnable world queries distributed in 3D space to improve query initialization and object coverage. To encourage cross-type interaction among heterogeneous queries, we introduce heterogeneous query mixing (QMix), which performs dedicated cross-type attention after feature sampling to consolidate complementary object evidence. We further propose interactive query swap sampling (QSwap), which improves feature sampling by allowing related queries to exchange informative feature tokens under attention and geometric constraints. Experiments on the nuScenes dataset show that ConFusion achieves state-of-the-art performance, reaching 59.1 mAP and 65.6 NDS on the validation set, and 61.6 mAP and 67.9 NDS on the test set.

</details>


### [41] [Refinement via Regeneration: Enlarging Modification Space Boosts Image Refinement in Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25636)
*Jiayi Guo,Linqing Wang,Jiangshan Wang,Yang Yue,Zeyu Liu,Zhiyuan Zhao,Qinglin Lu,Gao Huang,Chunyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"通过再生进行优化（RvR）"的新框架，将文本到图像的优化任务重新定义为条件图像再生而非编辑，通过利用目标提示和初始图像的语义标记作为条件，实现了更完整的语义对齐和更大的修改空间，在多个基准测试中取得了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型（UMMs）虽然能够通过编辑指令对初始生成的图像进行优化，但现有基于"通过编辑进行优化（RvE）"范式的方法存在两个关键局限：（1）编辑指令通常只能粗略地描述提示-图像不对齐问题，导致优化不完整；（2）像素级的内容保留虽然对编辑有必要，但过度限制了优化的有效修改空间。

Method: 提出"通过再生进行优化（RvR）"框架，将优化任务重新定义为条件图像再生。具体而言，RvR不使用编辑指令和严格的内容保留约束，而是基于目标提示和初始图像的语义标记来再生图像，从而实现更灵活的优化。

Result: 在多个基准测试上取得显著提升：Geneval从0.78提升至0.91，DPGBench从84.02提升至87.21，UniGenBench++从61.53提升至77.41。

Conclusion: RvR通过将优化重新定义为条件再生而非编辑，克服了现有RvE方法的局限性，在文本到图像生成优化任务中展现出优越的性能，为统一多模态模型的图像优化提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) integrate visual understanding and generation within a single framework. For text-to-image (T2I) tasks, this unified capability allows UMMs to refine outputs after their initial generation, potentially extending the performance upper bound. Current UMM-based refinement methods primarily follow a refinement-via-editing (RvE) paradigm, where UMMs produce editing instructions to modify misaligned regions while preserving aligned content. However, editing instructions often describe prompt-image misalignment only coarsely, leading to incomplete refinement. Moreover, pixel-level preservation, though necessary for editing, unnecessarily restricts the effective modification space for refinement. To address these limitations, we propose Refinement via Regeneration (RvR), a novel framework that reformulates refinement as conditional image regeneration rather than editing. Instead of relying on editing instructions and enforcing strict content preservation, RvR regenerates images conditioned on the target prompt and the semantic tokens of the initial image, enabling more complete semantic alignment with a larger modification space. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of RvR, improving Geneval from 0.78 to 0.91, DPGBench from 84.02 to 87.21, and UniGenBench++ from 61.53 to 77.41.

</details>


### [42] [Prefill-Time Intervention for Mitigating Hallucination in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25642)
*Chengsheng Zhang,Chenghao Sun,Xinyan Jiang,Wei Li,Xinmei Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出预填充阶段干预(PTI)方法，通过在预填充阶段一次性干预KV缓存来减少大视觉语言模型(LVLMs)的幻觉问题，相比传统的解码阶段干预方法能更有效地防止错误累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大视觉语言模型(LVLMs)在视觉-文本理解方面取得了显著进展，但其可靠性受到幻觉问题的严重影响。现有基于转向向量的方法虽然能减少幻觉，但会意外加剧残余幻觉的严重程度。作者认为这是因为现有方法仅关注解码阶段，而错误会在自回归过程中累积并逐渐恶化后续的幻觉输出。

Method: 提出Prefill-Time Intervention (PTI)，一种新颖的转向范式：1) 仅在预填充阶段进行一次干预，在错误累积发生之前增强初始Key-Value (KV)缓存；2) 模态感知设计，为视觉和文本表示推导不同的方向；3) 解耦干预：将keys导向视觉基础对象，values过滤背景噪声，从源头纠正易产生幻觉的表示。

Result: 大量实验表明PTI在减轻幻觉方面具有显著性能，并在不同解码策略、LVLMs和基准测试中具有良好的泛化性。此外，PTI与现有解码阶段方法正交，可实现即插即用集成并进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: PTI通过在预填充阶段从源头干预KV缓存，有效解决了传统解码阶段干预方法导致的错误累积问题，为减少LVLMs幻觉提供了一种更有效、更通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have achieved remarkable progress in visual-textual understanding, yet their reliability is critically undermined by hallucinations, i.e., the generation of factually incorrect or inconsistent responses. While recent studies using steering vectors demonstrated promise in reducing hallucinations, a notable challenge remains: they inadvertently amplify the severity of residual hallucinations. We attribute this to their exclusive focus on the decoding stage, where errors accumulate autoregressively and progressively worsen subsequent hallucinatory outputs. To address this, we propose Prefill-Time Intervention (PTI), a novel steering paradigm that intervenes only once during the prefill stage, enhancing the initial Key-Value (KV) cache before error accumulation occurs. Specifically, PTI is modality-aware, deriving distinct directions for visual and textual representations. This intervention is decoupled to steer keys toward visually-grounded objects and values to filter background noise, correcting hallucination-prone representations at their source. Extensive experiments demonstrate PTI's significant performance in mitigating hallucinations and its generalizability across diverse decoding strategies, LVLMs, and benchmarks. Moreover, PTI is orthogonal to existing decoding-stage methods, enabling plug-and-play integration and further boosting performance. Code is available at: https://github.com/huaiyi66/PTI.

</details>


### [43] [Exploring Remote Photoplethysmography for Neonatal Pain Detection from Facial Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25680)
*Ashutosh Dhamaniya,Anup Kumar Gupta,Trishna Saikia,Puneet Gupta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于rPPG的非接触式新生儿疼痛检测方法，通过质量参数和信噪比优化信号提取，并证明rPPG与音频特征融合可获得最佳检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新生儿未处理的疼痛会导致发育迟缓和体重增长缓慢等不良影响，因此需要更客观可靠的疼痛评估方法。传统接触式生理参数监测方法不适合长期监测，且增加疾病传播风险（如COVID-19）。

Method: 提出一种基于远程光电容积脉搏波描记法（rPPG）的非接触式脉搏信号估计方法用于新生儿疼痛检测。引入质量参数选择受皮肤变形影响最小的感兴趣区域（ROI）时序信号，并采用信噪比作为适应度参数提取受噪声影响最小的rPPG信号。同时结合rPPG特征与音频特征进行多模态融合。

Result: 实验结果表明rPPG信号可为新生儿疼痛检测提供有效信息；蓝色通道提取的信号性能优于其他颜色通道；rPPG与音频特征融合的结果优于单一模态方法。

Conclusion: 基于rPPG的非接触式方法能够有效用于新生儿疼痛检测，多模态特征融合（rPPG+音频）可进一步提升检测性能，为新生儿重症监护室（NICU）提供了一种安全、客观的疼痛评估替代方案。

Abstract: Unaddressed pain in neonates can lead to adverse effects, including delayed development and slower weight gain, emphasising the need for more objective and reliable pain assessment methods. Hence, automated methods using behavioural and physiological pain indicators have been developed to aid healthcare professionals in the Neonatal ICU. Traditional contact-based methods for physiological parameter estimation are unsuitable for long-term monitoring and increase the risk of spreading diseases like COVID-19. We introduce a novel approach using remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) to estimate pulse signals in a non-contact manner and employ them for neonatal pain detection. The temporal signals acquired from regions-of-interest (ROIs) affected by skin deformations may exhibit lower quality and provide erroneous rPPG signals. Therefore, we incorporated a quality parameter to select the temporal signals obtained from ROIs that are least affected by skin deformations. Further, we employed signal-to-noise ratio as a fitness parameter to extract the rPPG signal corresponding to the clip that is least affected by noise. Experimental findings demonstrate that the rPPG signals provide useful information for neonatal pain detection, and signals extracted from the blue colour channel outperform those extracted from other colour channels. We also show that combining rPPG and audio features provides better results than individual modalities.

</details>


### [44] [Toward Multimodal Conversational AI for Age-Related Macular Degeneration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25720)
*Ran Gu,Benjamin Hou,Mélanie Hébert,Asmita Indurkar,Yifan Yang,Emily Y. Chew,Tiarnán D. L. Keenan,Zhiyong Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OcularChat是一个基于Qwen2.5-VL微调的多模态大语言模型，通过模拟医患对话训练，用于年龄相关性黄斑变性(AMD)的诊断和视觉问答，在AREDS和AREDS2数据集上显著优于现有MLLMs，并能提供临床可解释的诊断推理和交互式对话。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型在视网膜疾病检测中表现强劲，但大多数系统仅产生静态预测，缺乏临床推理或交互式解释能力。多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)的最新进展可将诊断预测与具有临床意义的对话相结合，以支持临床决策和患者咨询。

Method: 开发OcularChat模型，基于Qwen2.5-VL进行微调；使用705,850个模拟医患对话（ paired with 46,167张彩色眼底照片CFPs）进行训练；通过视觉问答方式诊断AMD；训练模型识别关键AMD特征并生成推理预测。

Result: 在AREDS数据集上，OcularChat在三个诊断任务中达到高准确率：晚期AMD(0.954)、色素异常(0.849)和玻璃膜疣大小(0.678)，显著优于现有MLLMs；在AREDS2数据集上，所有任务均表现最佳；在5分临床评分量表中，三位独立眼科医生的主观评估显示OcularChat在晚期AMD(3.503 vs 2.833)、色素异常(3.272 vs 2.828)、玻璃膜疣大小(3.064 vs 2.433)和总体印象(2.978 vs 2.464)上均优于强基线模型。

Conclusion: MLLMs能够实现准确、可解释且临床上有用的基于图像的AMD诊断和分类。OcularChat不仅在AMD严重程度分类中表现出强大的客观性能，还能提供诊断推理、临床相关解释和交互式对话，在主观眼科医生评估中表现优异。

Abstract: Despite strong performance of deep learning models in retinal disease detection, most systems produce static predictions without clinical reasoning or interactive explanation. Recent advances in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) integrate diagnostic predictions with clinically meaningful dialogue to support clinical decision-making and patient counseling. In this study, OcularChat, an MLLM, was fine-tuned from Qwen2.5-VL using simulated patient-physician dialogues to diagnose age-related macular degeneration (AMD) through visual question answering on color fundus photographs (CFPs). A total of 705,850 simulated dialogues paired with 46,167 CFPs were generated to train OcularChat to identify key AMD features and produce reasoned predictions. OcularChat demonstrated strong classification performance in AREDS, achieving accuracies of 0.954, 0.849, and 0.678 for the three diagnostic tasks: advanced AMD, pigmentary abnormalities, and drusen size, significantly outperforming existing MLLMs. On AREDS2, OcularChat remained the top-performing method on all tasks. Across three independent ophthalmologist graders, OcularChat achieved higher mean scores than a strong baseline model for advanced AMD (3.503 vs. 2.833), pigmentary abnormalities (3.272 vs. 2.828), drusen size (3.064 vs. 2.433), and overall impression (2.978 vs. 2.464) on a 5-point clinical grading rubric. Beyond strong objective performance in AMD severity classification, OcularChat demonstrated the ability to provide diagnostic reasoning, clinically relevant explanations, and interactive dialogue, with high performance in subjective ophthalmologist evaluation. These findings suggest that MLLMs may enable accurate, interpretable, and clinically useful image-based diagnosis and classification of AMD.

</details>


### [45] [Sketch2Arti: Sketch-based Articulation Modeling of CAD Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25781)
*Yi Yang,Hao Pan,Yijing Cui,Alla Sheffer,Changjian Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Sketch2Arti 是首个基于草图的 CAD 物体关节建模系统，允许用户通过 2D 草图指定运动意图，自动识别可动部件并预测运动参数，且以类别无关方式训练，具有强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关节建模旨在推断 3D 物体的可动部件及其运动参数，以支持交互式动画、模拟和形状编辑。现有方法中，设计师通常通过轻量级草图（如箭头和笔画）自然地表达关节运动意图，但将这些草图转换为关节 3D 模型的过程仍然主要依赖手工操作，效率低下。因此，需要一种能够自动将用户草图映射为 3D 关节模型的方法，以降低建模门槛并提升效率。

Method: 提出 Sketch2Arti 系统，核心方法包括：(1) 允许用户从选定视角绘制简单的 2D 草图来表达关节运动意图；(2) 基于 CAD 模型和用户草图，自动发现对应可动部件并预测其运动参数；(3) 支持对复杂物体进行多次关节建模的迭代建模与细粒度控制；(4) 采用类别无关的训练策略，不依赖物体类别信息；(5) 针对缺乏内部结构的壳体模型，支持由用户草图引导的可控内部补全，生成与现有几何结构和预测运动约束一致的合理内部组件。

Result: 大量实验和用户评估验证了 Sketch2Arti 在有效性、可控性和泛化性方面的优势。该系统能够处理复杂物体上的多个关节，且对现有数据集之外的多样化物体具有强泛化能力。

Conclusion: Sketch2Arti 成功填补了草图与 3D 关节模型之间的鸿沟，提供了一种直观、可控且泛化能力强的关节建模解决方案。其类别无关的训练方式和内部补全能力进一步扩展了适用范围，为 CAD 物体的交互式动画、模拟和形状编辑提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Articulation modeling aims to infer movable parts and their motion parameters for a 3D object, enabling interactive animation, simulation, and shape editing. In this paper, we present Sketch2Arti, the first sketch-based articulation modeling system for CAD objects. Our key observation is that designers naturally communicate articulation intent through lightweight sketches (e.g., arrows and strokes) that indicate how parts should move, yet translating such sketches into articulated 3D models remains largely manual. Sketch2Arti bridges this gap by enabling users to specify articulation through simple 2D sketches drawn from a chosen viewpoint. Given a CAD model and user sketches, our approach automatically discovers the corresponding movable parts and predicts their motion parameters, allowing iterative modeling of multiple articulations on complex objects with fine-grained control. Importantly, Sketch2Arti is trained in a category-agnostic manner without requiring object category information, leading to strong generalization to diverse objects beyond existing articulation datasets. Moreover, for shell models lacking interior structures, Sketch2Arti supports controllable internal completion guided by user sketches, generating plausible internal components consistent with the existing geometry and predicted motion constraints. Comprehensive experiments and user evaluations demonstrate the effectiveness, controllability, and generalization of Sketch2Arti. The code, dataset, and the prototype system are at https://arlo-yang.github.io/Sketch2Arti.

</details>


### [46] [SIEVES: Selective Prediction Generalizes through Visual Evidence Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25855)
*Hector G. Rodriguez,Marcus Rohrbach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SIEVES通过让推理模型生成视觉证据并训练选择器评估证据质量，实现了多模态大语言模型在分布外场景下的高效选择性预测，覆盖率提升达三倍，且可迁移至黑盒专有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在视觉-语言任务上表现日益增强，传统视觉问答基准接近饱和，但在真实世界的分布外（OOD）场景中，可靠部署仍需满足低错误容忍度。选择性预测（selective prediction）旨在提高覆盖率（系统回答的输入比例），同时遵守用户定义的风险水平。现有方法通常通过为每个答案分配置信度分数并在低于阈值时放弃回答，但在OOD场景下的泛化能力仍不足。

Method: 提出SIEVES（Selective Prediction through Visual Evidence Scoring）框架。该方法要求推理模型（reasoner）在回答时生成局部化的视觉证据（localized visual evidence），并设计一个选择器（selector）显式学习估计推理器提供的定位质量。选择器通过评估视觉证据的质量来进行选择性预测，而非仅依赖模型的置信度分数。

Result: 在五个具有挑战性的OOD基准测试（V* Bench、HR-Bench-8k、MME-RealWorld-Lite、VizWiz和AdVQA）上，SIEVES相比非定位基线将覆盖率提高了最多三倍。此外，SIEVES选择器的设计使其能够迁移到无法访问权重或logits的专有推理模型（如o3和Gemini-3-Pro），提供的覆盖率提升超越了仅由准确性带来的提升。SIEVES在所有五个测试的OOD数据集和推理模型（Pixel-Reasoner、o3和Gemini-3-Pro）上均实现泛化，无需针对特定基准或推理模型的训练或适配。

Conclusion: SIEVES通过利用局部化视觉证据进行选择性预测，显著提升了MLLMs在OOD场景下的覆盖率和可靠性。该方法具有良好的跨数据集和跨模型泛化能力，且可应用于黑盒专有模型，为实现更可靠的视觉问答系统部署提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) achieve ever-stronger performance on visual-language tasks. Even as traditional visual question answering benchmarks approach saturation, reliable deployment requires satisfying low error tolerances in real-world out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. Precisely, selective prediction aims to improve coverage, i.e. the share of inputs the system answers, while adhering to a user-defined risk level. This is typically achieved by assigning a confidence score to each answer and abstaining on those that fall below a certain threshold. To enable reliable generalization, we require reasoner models to produce localized visual evidence while answering, and design a selector that explicitly learns to estimate the quality of the localization provided by the reasoner. We show that SIEVES (Selective Prediction through Visual Evidence Scoring) improves coverage by up to three times on challenging OOD benchmarks (V* Bench, HR-Bench-8k, MME-RealWorld-Lite, VizWiz, and AdVQA), compared to non-grounding baselines. Beyond better generalization to OOD tasks, the design of the SIEVES selector enables transfer to proprietary reasoners without access to their weights or logits, such as o3 and Gemini-3-Pro, providing coverage boosts beyond those attributable to accuracy alone. We highlight that SIEVES generalizes across all five tested OOD datasets and reasoner models (Pixel-Reasoner, o3, and Gemini-3-Pro), without benchmark- or reasoner-specific training or adaptation.

</details>


### [47] [Instruction-Evidence Contrastive Dual-Stream Decoding for Grounded Vision-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25809)
*Yashwant Pravinrao Bangde,Debaditya Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IECD2解码框架，通过并行维护指令驱动流和证据驱动流，并利用对称KL散度对比门控自适应融合，有效缓解视觉语言模型中的幻觉问题，在多个基准数据集上实现了任务准确率和视觉忠实度的双重提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLMs）在指令遵循和开放式视觉语言推理中表现优异，但常生成与视觉证据弱相关的流畅输出。现有研究表明，指令提示会进一步放大语言先验，尤其在视觉信号不确定或模糊时加剧幻觉问题。因此，需要一种能显式平衡语言信息量与视觉忠实度的解码方法。

Method: 提出Instruction-Evidence Contrastive Dual-Stream Decoding (IECD2)解码框架。该方法在每一步解码时并行维护两个概率分布：指令驱动流（促进表达丰富、信息充分的响应）和证据驱动流（强制严格基于图像 grounding）。两个流通过对称KL散度（symmetric KL-based contrast-based gate）进行自适应融合，抑制受语言先验偏好但缺乏视觉证据支持的token，同时在两个分布一致时保留这些token。

Result: 在POPE、MME、VQAv2、AMBER、MS-COCO和LLaVA-Bench等多个涵盖图像描述和视觉问答等生成式视觉语言推理任务的数据集上进行评估。IECD2在任务准确率和推理性能上实现持续提升，并在所有评估指标上相比当前最优解码方法显著降低幻觉率。

Conclusion: IECD2通过显式对比指令驱动与证据驱动的token分布，有效抑制了语言先验导致的幻觉，实现了语言信息量与视觉忠实度的自适应平衡，为提升视觉语言模型的可靠性和准确性提供了一种有效的解码范式。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) exhibit strong performance in instruction following and open-ended vision-language reasoning, yet they frequently generate fluent outputs that are weakly grounded in visual evidence. Prior works have shown that instruction prompting further worsens this issue by amplifying language priors, especially when the visual signal is uncertain or ambiguous. To address this challenge, we propose a decoding framework that explicitly balances linguistic informativeness and visual faithfulness during generation. Our method, Instruction-Evidence Contrastive Dual-Stream Decoding (IECD2), maintains two parallel probability distributions of tokens at each decoding step: an instruction-driven stream that promotes expressive and informative responses, and an evidence-driven stream that enforces strict grounding in the image. These two streams are adaptively fused using a symmetric KL-based contrast-based gate, which suppresses tokens favored by language priors but unsupported by visual evidence, while preserving them when both distributions agree. We evaluate IECD2 on multiple datasets spanning various generative vision-language reasoning tasks such as captioning and visual question answering, including POPE, MME, VQAv2, AMBER, MS-COCO, and LLaVA-Bench. IECD2 demonstrates consistent improvements in task accuracy and reasoning performance, alongside a substantial reduction in hallucination across all evaluation metrics compared to state-of-the-art decoding approaches.

</details>


### [48] [Magnification-Invariant Image Classification via Domain Generalization and Stable Sparse Embedding Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25817)
*Ifeanyi Ezuma,Olusiji Medaiyese*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出梯度反转域泛化模型解决组织病理学中的放大倍数偏移问题，在BreaKHis数据集上实现了更优的跨尺度泛化、更低的Brier分数、更紧凑（降维3倍）且可复现的特征表示，优于GAN增强和监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放大倍数偏移是组织病理学分类鲁棒性的主要障碍，因为在一个成像尺度上训练的模型往往难以泛化到另一个尺度。现有方法（如数据增强或域适应）在解决此问题时效果不稳定，亟需一种能够学习可迁移且紧凑表示的方法。

Method: 在BreaKHis数据集上，采用严格的患者不相交留一放大倍数交叉验证协议，比较了三种方法：（1）监督学习基线；（2）基线结合DCGAN生成图像块进行数据增强；（3）梯度反转域泛化模型——该模型通过对抗训练在保留判别信息的同时抑制放大倍数特异性变异。

Result: 域泛化模型在跨放大倍数测试中整体判别性能最强，尤其在200X被留出时提升最明显。GAN增强效果不一致，部分折提升、部分折下降（特别是400X）。域泛化模型的Brier分数最低（0.063 vs 基线0.089）。稀疏嵌入分析显示，域泛化训练将平均特征签名维度降低3倍以上（306 vs 1,074维），同时保持等效的预测性能（AUC: 0.967 vs 0.965；F1: 0.930 vs 0.931）。此外，跨折特征签名的Jaccard重叠从基线的近零提升至100X与200X折之间的0.99。

Conclusion: 无需增加架构复杂度，即可学习到校准良好、紧凑且可迁移的表示，这对计算病理学模型在异质采集环境下的可靠部署具有明确意义。

Abstract: Magnification shift is a major obstacle to robust histopathology classification, because models trained on one imaging scale often generalize poorly to another. Here, we evaluated this problem on the BreaKHis dataset using a strict patient-disjoint leave-one-magnification-out protocol, comparing supervised baseline, baseline augmented with DCGAN-generated patches, and a gradient-reversal domain-general model designed to preserve discriminative information while suppressing magnification-specific variation. Across held-out magnifications, the domain-general model achieved the strongest overall discrimination and its clearest gain was observed when 200X was held out. By contrast, GAN augmentation produced inconsistent effects, improving some folds but degrading others, particularly at 400X. The domain-general model also yielded the lowest Brier score at 0.063 vs 0.089 at baseline. Sparse embedding analysis further revealed that domain-general training reduced average signature size more than three-fold (306 versus 1,074 dimensions) while preserving equivalent predictive performance (AUC: 0.967 vs 0.965; F1: 0.930 vs 0.931). It also increased cross-fold signature reproducibility from near-zero Jaccard overlap in the baseline to 0.99 between the 100X and 200X folds. These findings show that calibrated, compact, and transferable representations can be learned without added architectural complexity, with clear implications for the reliable deployment of computational pathology models across heterogeneous acquisition settings.

</details>


### [49] [Mutual Forcing: Dual-Mode Self-Evolution for Fast Autoregressive Audio-Video Character Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25819)
*Yupeng Zhou,Lianghua Huang,Zhifan Wu,Jiabao Wang,Yupeng Shi,Biao Jiang,Daquan Zhou,Yu Liu,Ming-Ming Cheng,Qibin Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Mutual Forcing 提出了一种无需双向教师模型的快速音视频自回归生成方法，通过在一个共享权重的因果模型中联合训练少步与多步生成模式实现自蒸馏，仅用 4-8 步即可达到 50 步基线的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在解决两个关键挑战：联合音视频建模与快速自回归生成。现有流式生成方法通常需要先训练双向模型，再通过多阶段蒸馏转换为因果生成器，流程复杂且开销大；同时，联合音视频优化也存在困难。

Method: 提出 Mutual Forcing 框架，采用两阶段训练策略：先训练单模态生成器，再耦合为统一音视频模型进行联合训练。核心创新在于直接从原生因果自回归模型出发，在一个权重共享的模型中集成少步生成与多步生成模式，实现自蒸馏并提升训练-推理一致性。多步模式通过自蒸馏提升少步模式，少步模式在训练时生成历史上下文以改善一致性，两种模式共享参数、相互强化。

Result: 实验表明，Mutual Forcing 在仅使用 4 到 8 步的情况下，达到或超过了需要约 50 步采样的强基线方法，在效率和质量上均展现出显著优势。相比 Self-Forcing 等方法，无需额外的双向教师模型，支持更灵活的训练序列长度，降低训练开销，并能直接从真实配对数据中提升性能。

Conclusion: Mutual Forcing 是一种高效的音视频联合生成框架，通过原生因果自回归模型中的双模式自蒸馏机制，实现了长程音视频同步的快速流式生成，在保持高质量的同时大幅减少了推理步数。

Abstract: In this work, we propose Mutual Forcing, a framework for fast autoregressive audio-video generation with long-horizon audio-video synchronization. Our approach addresses two key challenges: joint audio-video modeling and fast autoregressive generation. To ease joint audio-video optimization, we adopt a two-stage training strategy: we first train uni-modal generators and then couple them into a unified audio-video model for joint training on paired data. For streaming generation, we ask whether a native fast causal audio-video model can be trained directly, instead of following existing streaming distillation pipelines that typically train a bidirectional model first and then convert it into a causal generator through multiple distillation stages. Our answer is Mutual Forcing, which builds directly on native autoregressive model and integrates few-step and multi-step generation within a single weight-shared model, enabling self-distillation and improved training-inference consistency. The multi-step mode improves the few-step mode via self-distillation, while the few-step mode generates historical context during training to improve training-inference consistency; because the two modes share parameters, these two effects reinforce each other within a single model. Compared with prior approaches such as Self-Forcing, Mutual Forcing removes the need for an additional bidirectional teacher model, supports more flexible training sequence lengths, reduces training overhead, and allows the model to improve directly from real paired data rather than a fixed teacher. Experiments show that Mutual Forcing matches or surpasses strong baselines that require around 50 sampling steps while using only 4 to 8 steps, demonstrating substantial advantages in both efficiency and quality. The project page is available at https://mutualforcing.github.io.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [50] [VISION-SLS: Safe Perception-Based Control from Learned Visual Representations via System Level Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24894)
*Antoine P. Leeman,Shuyu Zhan,Melanie N. Zeilinger,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VISION-SLS通过预训练视觉特征的低维观测映射与SLS优化的输出反馈策略，结合可扩展的非凸求解器，实现了高分辨率图像下的安全视觉运动控制，并在仿真与硬件上验证了其有效性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的非线性输出反馈控制在处理高分辨率RGB图像时难以兼顾可扩展性与鲁棒约束满足保证，尤其在部分可观测、传感器噪声和非线性动力学等挑战下，缺乏实用的安全视觉运动控制方法。

Method: 提出VISION-SLS方法，核心包括：(i) 基于预训练视觉特征学习低维观测映射，并建立状态相关的误差界；(ii) 通过系统层级综合(SLS)优化因果仿射时变输出反馈策略。为此开发了基于序贯凸规划与高效Riccati递归的非凸规划可扩展求解器。

Result: 在模拟环境中验证了4D车辆、10D四旋翼（≥512×512像素）及59D人形机器人（部分可观测）任务，实现了安全的信息采集行为以降低不确定性并保证约束满足；在真实地面车辆硬件上验证了安全性优于基线方法，且求解时间更短。

Conclusion: 学习得到的视觉抽象与高效求解器相结合，使基于SLS的安全视觉运动输出反馈控制在大规模实际系统中变得可行。

Abstract: We propose VISION-SLS, a method for nonlinear output-feedback control from high-resolution RGB images which provides robust constraint satisfaction guarantees under calibrated uncertainty bounds despite partial observability, sensor noise, and nonlinear dynamics. To enable scalability while retaining guarantees, we propose: (i) a learned low-dimensional observation map from pretrained visual features with state-dependent error bounds, and (ii) a causal affine time-varying output-feedback policy optimized via System Level Synthesis (SLS). We develop a scalable, novel solver for the resulting nonconvex program that leverages sequential convex programming coupled with efficient Riccati recursions. On two simulated visuomotor tasks (a 4D car and a 10D quadrotor) with >= 512 x 512 pixels and a 59D humanoid task with partial observability, our method enables safe, information-gathering behavior that reduces uncertainty while guaranteeing constraint satisfaction with empirically-calibrated error bounds. We also validate our method on hardware, safely controlling a ground vehicle from onboard images, outperforming baselines in safety rate and solve times. Together, these results show that learned visual abstractions coupled with an efficient solver make SLS-based safe visuomotor output-feedback practical at scale. The code implementation of our method is available at https://github.com/trustworthyrobotics/VISION-SLS.

</details>


### [51] [An analysis of sensor selection for fruit picking with suction-based grippers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24906)
*Eva Krueger,Marcus Rosette,Joseph R. Davidson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文设计了一种集成到柔性吸盘式苹果采摘器中的多模态传感套件，通过识别采摘不同阶段最具信息量的传感器，实现了对成功采摘和即将发生的失败的预测性检测，在真实苹果园实验中达到90%以上的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人水果采摘中，可靠检测水果是否成功采摘是一个难题。由于水果和夹持器的柔顺性、茎秆附着力的变化以及果园环境中的遮挡，采摘状态估计困难。现有研究虽然探索了基于视觉的感知和多传感器学习方法，但用于准确采摘和滑动检测的最小传感器集以及阶段性传感策略仍未得到充分探索。

Method: 设计并评估了一种集成到柔性吸盘式苹果夹持器中的多模态传感套件。该方法识别了采摘不同阶段最具信息量的传感器，采用阶段性传感策略。使用随机森林（Random Forest）和多层感知器（Multilayer Perceptron）分类器进行采摘状态分类和事件预测。

Result: 在真实苹果园中的实验表明：随机森林和多层感知器分类器对成功采摘和即将发生的失败的检测准确率超过90%；随机森林能在人工标注真值0.09秒内预测采摘/滑动事件；识别出了可靠采摘状态分类所需的最小传感器集。

Conclusion: 通过阶段性多模态传感策略，可以在使用最小传感器集的情况下实现高精度的采摘状态分类和失败预测，为机器人水果采摘的可靠性提升提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Robotic fruit harvesting often fails to reliably detect whether a fruit has been successfully picked, limiting efficiency and increasing crop damage. This problem is difficult due to compliant fruit and grippers, variable stem attachment, and occlusions in orchard environments. Prior work has explored vision-based perception and multi-sensor learning approaches for pick state estimation. However, minimal sensor sets and phase-dependent sensing strategies for accurate pick and slip detection remain largely unexplored. In this work, we design and evaluate a multimodal sensing suite integrated into a compliant suction-based apple gripper. Our approach is unique because it identifies which sensors are most informative at different phases of the pick, enabling predictive detection of failures before they occur. The contributions of this paper are a phase-dependent evaluation of multimodal sensors and the identification of minimal sensor sets for reliable pick state classification. Experiments in a real apple orchard show that Random Forest and Multilayer Perceptron classifiers detect successful picks and impending failures with over 90% accuracy, and Random Forest predicts pick/slip events within 0.09 s of human-annotated ground truth.

</details>


### [52] [asRoBallet: Closing the Sim2Real Gap via Friction-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Underactuated Spherical Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24916)
*Fang Wan,Guangyi Huang,Tianyu Wu,Zishang Zhang,Bangchao Huang,Haoran Sun,Mingdong Chen,Chaoyang Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了asRoBallet，首个在仿人球轮机器人硬件上成功部署强化学习（RL）的研究。通过高保真MuJoCo仿真建模ETH型全向轮的离散滚轮力学，并开发摩擦感知强化学习框架，实现了零样本Sim2Real迁移，解决了球轮机器人在接触建模、执行器延迟和抖动以及安全硬件探索方面的关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球轮机器人是欠驱动和非完整约束控制的经典基准，但存在复杂摩擦模型（轮-球-地面交互）导致的仿真与现实差距。现有文献虽展示了基于LQR和MPC的3D平衡控制，但将强化学习应用于仿人球轮机器人硬件时，面临接触建模不足、执行器延迟与抖动、以及安全硬件探索等关键瓶颈。

Method: 1) 构建高保真MuJoCo仿真环境，显式建模ETH型全向轮的离散滚轮力学，捕捉此前被忽略的寄生振动和接触不连续性；2) 开发摩擦感知强化学习（Friction-Aware RL）框架，通过掌握轮-球和球-地面界面的耦合滚动、横向和扭转摩擦通道，实现零样本仿真到现实迁移；3) 采用减材重构方法设计asRoBallet硬件平台，复用过约束四足机器人的关键组件并集成到新设计的结构框架中，以低成本实现鲁棒的研究平台；4) 开发通用iOS生态系统，将消费电子产品转化为低延迟接口，使单一操作员能够通过直观的自然动作编排富有表现力的仿人动作。

Result: 首次在仿人球轮机器人硬件上成功部署强化学习，实现了零样本Sim2Real迁移，能够在实际硬件上完成富有表现力的仿人球轮动作控制。

Conclusion: 通过高保真接触建模和摩擦感知强化学习框架，成功克服了球轮机器人从仿真到现实的迁移难题，为欠驱动非完整约束系统的RL硬件部署提供了可行方案，同时展示了低成本、模块化的硬件设计思路。

Abstract: We introduce asRoBallet, to the best of our knowledge, the first successful deployment of reinforcement learning (RL) on a humanoid ballbot hardware. Historically, ballbots have served as a canonical benchmark for underactuated and nonholonomic control, which are characterized by a reality gap in complex friction models for wheel-sphere-ground interactions. While current literature demonstrates successful handling of 3D balancing with LQR and MPC, transitioning to actual hardware for a humanoid ballbot using RL is currently hindered by critical gaps in contact modeling, actuator latency & jitter, and safe hardware exploration, and safe hardware exploration. This study proposes a high-fidelity MuJoCo simulation that explicitly models the discrete roller mechanics of ETH-type omni-wheels, thereby capturing parasitic vibrations and contact discontinuities that are previously ignored. We also developed a Friction-Aware Reinforcement Learning framework that achieves zero-shot Sim2Real transfer by mastering the coupled rolling, lateral, and torsional friction channels at the wheel-sphere and sphere-ground interfaces. We designed asRoBallet through subtractive reconfiguration, repurposing key components from an overconstrained quadruped and integrating them into a newly designed structural frame to achieve a robust research platform at low cost. We also developed a generalized iOS ecosystem that transforms consumer electronics into a low-latency interface, enabling a single operator to orchestrate expressive humanoid maneuvers via intuitive natural motion.

</details>


### [53] [Libra-VLA: Achieving Learning Equilibrium via Asynchronous Coarse-to-Fine Dual-System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24921)
*Yifei Wei,Linqing Zhong,Yi Liu,Yuxiang Lu,Xindong He,Maoqing Yao,Guanghui Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Libra-VLA，一种粗到细的双系统VLA架构，通过混合动作空间将机器人操控分解为离散宏观方向和连续微观位姿对齐，解决单一生成范式中语义-执行差距过大的问题，并发现性能与动作分解粒度呈倒U型关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型采用单一生成范式，将视觉-语言特征直接映射到高频电机指令，这种扁平非层级方式忽略了机器人操控的固有层次结构，导致语义-执行差距过大，给高层语义到连续动作的映射带来沉重的表征负担。

Method: 提出Libra-VLA粗到细双系统架构：(1)语义规划器预测离散动作token捕获宏观方向意图；(2)动作细化器基于粗粒度意图生成高频连续动作实现精确对齐；(3)采用异步执行策略提升可扩展性和响应性；(4)在混合动作空间中分解复杂动作为离散宏观到达和连续微观位姿对齐。

Result: 发现性能相对于动作分解粒度呈倒U型曲线，当两个子系统间学习难度达到平衡时性能最优；异步设计为开放世界操控提供了可扩展、鲁棒且响应迅速的解决方案。

Conclusion: 通过显式解耦学习复杂度为粗到细层级结构，Libra-VLA实现了训练均衡，证明了层次化分解和异步执行在VLA模型中的有效性，为通用机器人操控提供了新的架构范式。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are a promising paradigm for generalist robotic manipulation by grounding high-level semantic instructions into executable physical actions. However, prevailing approaches typically adopt a monolithic generation paradigm, directly mapping visual-linguistic features to high-frequency motor commands in a flat, non-hierarchical fashion. This strategy overlooks the inherent hierarchy of robotic manipulation, where complex actions can be naturally modeled in a Hybrid Action Space, decomposing into discrete macro-directional reaching and continuous micro-pose alignment, severely widening the semantic-actuation gap and imposing a heavy representational burden on grounding high-level semantics to continuous actions. To address this, we introduce Libra-VLA, a novel Coarse-to-Fine Dual-System VLA architecture. We explicitly decouple the learning complexity into a coarse-to-fine hierarchy to strike a training equilibrium, while simultaneously leveraging this structural modularity to implement an asynchronous execution strategy. The Semantic Planner predicts discrete action tokens capturing macro-directional intent, while the Action Refiner conditions on coarse intent to generate high-frequency continuous actions for precise alignment. Crucially, our empirical analysis reveals that performance follows an inverted-U curve relative to action decomposition granularity, peaking exactly when the learning difficulty is balanced between the two sub-systems. With the asynchronous design, our approach offers a scalable, robust, and responsive solution for open-world manipulation.

</details>


### [54] [TEACar: An Open-Source Autonomous Driving Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24934)
*Zhongzheng Zhang,Maxwell Ruyle,Andrew Kappes,Tyler Ruble,William Shaoul,Dana Moreno,Jack Penn,Ivan Ruchkin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了TEACAR，一种1/14至1/16比例的小型模块化自动驾驶平台，采用四层甲板结构和ROS 2软件架构，通过硬件在环验证解决了现有小型自主赛车平台模块化不足、集成复杂和扩展性受限的问题，实验表明其在ITS研究、教育和开发中具有可扩展性、模块化和成本效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能交通系统（ITS）日益依赖基于视觉的感知和基于学习的控制，需要支持真实硬件在环验证的实验平台。现有的小型自主赛车平台虽然为硬件验证提供了实用途径，但普遍存在模块化程度低、集成复杂度高或扩展性受限等问题。

Method: 设计了TEACAR平台，采用1/14至1/16比例，具备模块化机械架构、硬件抽象和基于ROS 2的软件系统。系统采用四层甲板结构，在物理上将感知、计算、执行和电源子系统解耦，以提高结构刚性并简化重新配置。基于三种基于CNN的转向控制器对原型机进行全面评估。

Result: 通过测量推理延迟、功耗和系统运行时间来评估计算能力和鲁棒性。实验表明TEACAR在机械稳定性、结构特性和软件性能方面表现良好，为ITS研究、教育和开发提供了一个可扩展、模块化和具有成本效益的测试平台。

Conclusion: TEACAR通过其模块化机械设计、硬件抽象和ROS 2软件架构，成功解决了现有小型自动驾驶平台的关键局限性，证明了其作为ITS研究、教育和开发的可扩展、模块化和成本效益测试平台的价值。项目代码已在GitHub开源。

Abstract: Intelligent Transportation Systems (ITS) increasingly rely on vision-based perception and learning-based control, necessitating experimental platforms that support realistic hardware-in-the-loop validation. Small-scale platforms for autonomous racing offer a practical path to hardware validation, but often suffer from limited modularity, high integration complexity, or restricted extensibility. This paper presents TEACAR, a 1/14- to 1/16-scale autonomous driving platform designed with modular mechanical architecture, hardware abstraction, and ROS 2-based software. The system adopts a four-layer deck structure that physically decouples sensing, computation, actuation, and power subsystems, improving structural rigidity while simplifying reconfiguration. We constructed and comprehensively evaluated the prototype of TEACAR. Its mechanical stability, structural characteristics, and software performance were quantified based on three CNN-based steering controllers. Inference latency, power consumption, and system operating time were measured to evaluate computational capability and robustness. Our experiments demonstrated that TEACAR offers a scalable, modular, and cost-effective testbed for ITS research, education, and development. Our project repository is available on GitHub.

</details>


### [55] [DiscreteRTC: Discrete Diffusion Policies are Natural Asynchronous Executors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25050)
*Pengcheng Wang,Kaiwen Hong,Chensheng Peng,Katherine Driggs-Campbell,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Chenfeng Xu,Chen Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DiscreteRTC，利用离散扩散策略的原生unmasking机制实现异步实时执行，无需额外代码即可在推理过程中动态修正动作，在动态模拟和真实机器人任务中表现优于基于流匹配的RTC方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理AI需要在动态环境中边行动边思考，而同步执行器的chunk间停顿对动态任务是致命的。现有基于流匹配的实时分块(RTC)方法存在结构性缺陷：inpainting依赖推理时的外部修正，导致预训练收益低、需要特定微调、依赖启发式引导且增加计算延迟。

Method: 提出DiscreteRTC，利用离散扩散策略（通过迭代unmasking生成动作）作为自然异步执行器。该方法将外部修正替换为策略原生的unmasking操作，并通过early stopping提供自适应引导并降低推理成本。

Result: 在动态模拟基准和真实动态操作任务上，DiscreteRTC比连续RTC及其他基线取得更高成功率。具体而言，异步inpainting实现零行代码；推理计算量仅为从头生成动作的0.7倍；在真实动态抓取任务中成功率比基于流匹配的RTC高50%。

Conclusion: 离散扩散策略是物理AI异步执行的自然选择，其原生unmasking机制同时解决了流匹配RTC的多个结构性限制。DiscreteRTC更简单、更快、更有效，为动态物理AI任务提供了优越的异步执行方案。

Abstract: Unlike chatbots, physical AI must act while the world keeps evolving. Therefore, the inter-chunk pause of synchronous executors are fatal for dynamic tasks regardless of how fast the inference is. Asynchronous execution -- thinking while acting -- is therefore a structural requirement, and real-time chunking (RTC) makes it viable by recasting chunk transitions as inpainting: freezing committed actions and consistently generating the remainder. However, RTC with flow-matching policy is structurally suboptimal: its inpainting comes from inference-time corrections rather than the base policy, yielding little pre-training benefit, specific fine-tuning, heuristic guidance, and extra computation that inflates the latency. In this work, we observe that discrete diffusion policies, which generate actions by iteratively unmasking, are natural asynchronous executors that resolve all limitations at once: they are fine-tuning free since inpainting is their native operation, while early stopping further provides adaptive guidance and reduces inference cost. We propose DiscreteRTC, which replaces external corrections with native unmasking, and show on dynamic simulated benchmarks and real-world dynamic manipulation tasks that it achieves higher success rates than continuous RTC and other baselines. In summary, DiscreteRTC is simpler to implement with 0 lines of code for async inpainting, faster at inference with only 0.7x computation compared with generating actions from scratch, and better at execution with 50% higher success rate in real-world dynamic pick task compared with flow-matching-based RTC. More visualizations are on https://outsider86.github.io/DiscreteRTCSite/.

</details>


### [56] [HANDFUL: Sequential Grasp-Conditioned Dexterous Manipulation with Resource Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25126)
*Ethan Foong,Yunshuang Li,Hao Jiang,Gaurav S. Sukhatme,Daniel Seita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HANDFUL框架，通过将手指使用建模为有限资源，学习资源感知抓取以实现序列化灵巧操作，并在仿真和真实LEAP手上验证了该方法在多功能操作任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧操作研究多聚焦于单物体、单技能任务，而实际序列化任务需要机器人在执行多个连续技能时保持对已抓取物体的控制，因此需要资源感知的抓取策略来保留手指资源供后续动作使用。

Method: 提出HANDFUL学习框架：(1) 将手指使用建模为有限资源，通过手指级接触奖励鼓励资源感知抓取的探索；(2) 通过基于课程的策略学习选择适合下游任务的抓取；(3) 构建HANDFUL-Bench仿真基准，包含推、拉、按等多种序列化灵巧操作任务。

Result: 大量仿真实验表明，优先选择资源感知抓取相比贪婪优化初始抓取的基线方法，显著提高了第二子任务的成功率和鲁棒性；方法在真实LEAP灵巧手上得到验证。

Conclusion: 资源感知抓取规划是多功能灵巧操作的关键原则，为序列化灵巧操作提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Dexterous robot hands offer rich opportunities for multifunctional manipulation, where a robot must execute multiple skills in sequence while maintaining control over previously grasped objects. Most prior work in dexterous manipulation focuses on single-object, single-skill tasks. In contrast, our insight is that many sequential tasks require resource-aware grasps that conserve fingers for future actions. In this paper, we study sequential grasp-conditioned dexterous manipulation, where a robot first grasps an object and then performs a second, distinct manipulation subtask while preserving the initial grasp. We introduce HANDFUL, a learning framework that models finger usage as a limited resource and encourages exploration of resource-aware grasps through finger-level contact rewards. These grasps are subsequently selected for downstream tasks via curriculum-based policy learning. We further propose HANDFUL-Bench, a simulation benchmark that introduces sequential dexterous manipulation tasks across multiple secondsubtask objectives, including pushing, pulling, and pressing, under a shared grasp-conditioned setup. Extensive simulation results demonstrate that prioritizing resource-aware grasps improves second-subtask success and robustness compared to a baseline that greedily optimizes the initial grasp before attempting the second subtask. We additionally validate our approach on a real dexterous LEAP hand. Together, this work establishes resource-aware grasp planning as a key principle for multifunctional dexterous manipulation. Supplementary material is available on our website: https://handful-dex.github.io.

</details>


### [57] [Slot-hopping Enabled Loiter Guidance and Automation for Fixed-wing UAV Corridors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25292)
*Pradeep J,Siddhardha Kedarisetty,Ashwini Ratnoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种半合作制导策略，通过直接会合或协调slot hopping的方式，将固定翼UAV高效、低干扰地插入loiter lane，从而实现更紧凑的UAV走廊，并通过仿真验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定翼无人机（UAV）走廊中的交通拥堵管理问题。现有loiter-lane框架需要进一步发展，以实现更紧凑的固定翼UAV走廊运行，同时减少对已在走廊中运行的无人机的干扰。

Method: 提出了一种半合作制导策略，用于将固定翼UAV插入loiter lane。该策略基于合作性和非干扰性loiter-lane插入概念，使 incoming UAV 首先尝试在其速度限制内与现有的空loiter slot会合；如果直接插入不可行，则让最少数量的正在loiter的UAV执行协调的slot hopping，以创建一个位置合适的空slot。

Result: 通过数值仿真验证了该方法的可行性和性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的半合作制导策略能够在最小干扰已有UAV的情况下，实现固定翼UAV向loiter lane的插入，并支持更紧凑的固定翼UAV走廊设计。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of traffic congestion management in fixed-wing unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) corridors by further developing a recently introduced loiter-lane framework. A semi-cooperative guidance strategy is developed for inserting fixed-wing UAVs into a loiter lane with minimal disruption to the UAVs already operating within it, while enabling a more compact fixed-wing UAV corridor. Building on the concepts of cooperative and non-disruptive loiter-lane insertion, the proposed strategy makes the incoming UAV first attempt, within its speed bounds, to rendezvous with an existing empty loiter slot. If direct insertion is infeasible, a minimal number of loitering UAVs perform coordinated slot hopping to create a suitably positioned empty slot. The feasibility and performance of the method are demonstrated through numerical simulations.

</details>


### [58] [ANCHOR: A Physically Grounded Closed-Loop Framework for Robust Home-Service Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25323)
*Jinhao Jiang,Shengyu Fang,Sibo Zuo,Yujie Tang,Yirui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 ANCHOR 框架，通过将符号推理与可验证的物理状态对齐，解决开放词汇移动操作机器人在长时程任务中因符号计划与物理世界不一致导致的执行失败问题，在真实机器人试验中将任务成功率从 53.3% 提升至 71.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇移动操作机器人已进入真实家庭环境，但现有系统在长时程执行中面临三类反复出现的失败：(i) 依赖预扫描语义地图，在场景变化和扰动后变得不一致；(ii) 选择导航终点时未考虑下游操作可行性，导致"到达但无法操作"问题；(iii) 通过无差别的全局重规划处理异常，无法有效遏制局部错误。这些失败的根源并非语义误解，而是符号计划与不断演变的物理世界之间的不一致。

Method: 提出 ANCHOR，一个物理 grounded 的闭环框架，包含三个核心机制：(i) 物理锚定任务规划——将符号谓词绑定到可观测的几何锚点，并在每次动作后重新验证；(ii) 可操作性感知的基座对齐——确保导航终点满足运动学可达性和局部碰撞可行性；(iii) 最小责任层分层恢复——在感知、基座-手臂协调和执行层之间定位故障，防止级联重试。

Result: 在 60 次真实机器人试验（此前未见环境）中，ANCHOR 将任务成功率从 53.3% 提升至 71.7%，在扰动下实现 71.4% 的恢复率，证明了显式物理 grounding 和结构化故障遏制对鲁棒移动操作的关键作用。

Conclusion: 显式物理 grounding 和结构化故障遏制是鲁棒移动操作的关键。通过将符号推理与可验证物理状态对齐，ANCHOR 有效解决了执行不一致问题，显著提升了开放词汇移动操作机器人在真实环境中的长时程任务成功率。

Abstract: Recent advances in open-vocabulary mobile manipulation have brought robots into real domestic environments. In such settings, reliable long-horizon execution under open-set object references and frequent disturbances becomes essential. However, many failures persist. These are not caused by semantic misunderstanding but by inconsistencies between symbolic plans and the evolving physical world, manifested as three recurring limitations: (i) existing systems often rely on pre-scanned semantic maps that become inconsistent after scene changes and disturbances; (ii) they select navigation endpoints without considering downstream manipulation feasibility, causing the "arrived but inoperable" problem; and (iii) they handle anomalies through undifferentiated global replanning, which often fails to contain local errors. To address this execution inconsistency, we present ANCHOR, a physically grounded closed-loop framework that aligns symbolic reasoning with verifiable physical state during execution. ANCHOR integrates three mechanisms: (i) physically anchored task planning, which binds symbolic predicates to observable geometric anchors and re-validates them after each action; (ii) operability-aware base alignment, which ensures that navigation endpoints satisfy kinematic reachability and local collision feasibility; and (iii) minimum-responsible-layer hierarchical recovery, which localizes failures across perception, base-arm coordination, and execution layers to prevent cascading retries. Across 60 real-robot trials in previously unseen environments, ANCHOR improves task success from 53.3% to 71.7% and achieves a 71.4% recovery rate under perturbations, demonstrating that explicit physical grounding and structured failure containment are critical for robust mobile manipulation. Our project page is available at https://anchor9178.github.io/ANCHOR/ .

</details>


### [59] [ProDrive: Proactive Planning for Autonomous Driving via Ego-Environment Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25329)
*Chuyao Fu,Shengzhe Gan,Zhuoli Ouyang,Yuhan Rui,Xiaowei Chi,Sirui Han,Jiankun Wang,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ProDrive是一种基于世界模型的主动规划框架，通过端到端联合训练轨迹规划器和BEV世界模型，实现自车-环境协同演化，使自动驾驶系统能够预测未来场景并做出主动规划决策，在NAVSIM v1上取得优异的安全性和规划效率表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶规划器仅基于当前观测生成轨迹，无法预测未来场景演化，导致短视决策和安全关键失败。真实世界驾驶高度动态，需要超越反应式规划的主动规划能力。

Method: 提出ProDrive，一种基于世界模型的主动规划框架，实现自车-环境协同演化。该方法端到端联合训练查询中心轨迹规划器和BEV世界模型：规划器生成多样化候选轨迹和规划感知自车token，世界模型基于这些条件预测未来场景演化。通过将规划器特征注入世界模型并并行评估所有候选轨迹，保持端到端梯度流，使未来结果评估直接塑造规划决策。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1上的实验表明，ProDrive在安全性和规划效率方面均优于强基线方法；消融实验验证了所提出的自车-环境耦合设计的有效性。

Conclusion: ProDrive通过双向耦合的规划器与世界模型，实现了超越当前观测驱动决策的主动规划，为自动驾驶中的自车-环境协同演化提供了有效框架。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving planners typically generate trajectories from current observations alone. However, real-world driving is highly dynamic, and such reactive planning cannot anticipate future scene evolution, often leading to myopic decisions and safety-critical failures. We propose ProDrive, a world-model-based proactive planning framework that enables ego-environment co-evolution for autonomous driving. ProDrive jointly trains a query-centric trajectory planner and a bird's-eye-view (BEV) world model end-to-end: the planner generates diverse candidate trajectories and planning-aware ego tokens, while the world model predicts future scene evolution conditioned on them. By injecting planner features into the world model and evaluating all candidates in parallel, ProDrive preserves end-to-end gradient flow and allows future outcome assessment to directly shape planning. This bidirectional coupling enables proactive planning beyond current-observation-driven decision-making. Experiments on NAVSIM v1 show that ProDrive outperforms strong baselines in both safety and planning efficiency, while ablations validate the effectiveness of the proposed ego-environment coupling design.

</details>

*David Perez-Saura,Jose Andres Millan-Romera,Miguel Fernandez-Cortizas,Holger Voos,Pascual Campoy,Jose Luis Sanchez-Lopez*

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种语义增强的图匹配方法，通过将RGB-D数据检测到的物体与结构元素（房间、墙面）的关系显式建模，在几何验证前过滤候选对应关系，显著降低了对称和重复室内环境中的定位歧义与搜索复杂度，并在iS-Graphs框架中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于场景的图表示（如场景图）可用于结构化室内环境中的定位，但在具有重复或对称布局的环境中，仅靠结构线索往往无法解决歧义，导致图匹配困难。

Method: 提出语义增强的图匹配方法：1）从RGB-D数据中检测物体并集成到图中；2）显式建模物体与结构元素（房间、墙面）之间的关系；3）利用这些语义关系在几何验证前过滤候选对应关系；4）将该方法集成到iS-Graphs框架中。

Result: 在合成和模拟环境中进行评估，结果表明语义关系显著减少了候选匹配数量，提高了计算效率并加快了收敛速度，尤其在纯几何方法失效的对称场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: 语义关系与结构元素的显式建模能够有效解决对称和重复环境中的定位歧义，语义增强的图匹配在计算效率和鲁棒性上均优于纯几何方法。

Abstract: Graph-based representations such as Scene Graphs enable localization in structured indoor environments by matching a locally observed graph, constructed from sensor data, to a prior map. This process is particularly challenging in environments with repetitive or symmetric layouts, where structural cues alone are often insufficient to resolve ambiguities. We propose a semantic-enhanced graph matching approach that explicitly models relations between detected objects and structural elements, such as rooms and wall planes. Objects are detected from RGB-D data and integrated into the graph, and their relations to structural elements are exploited to filter candidate correspondences prior to geometric verification, significantly reducing ambiguity and search complexity. The proposed method is integrated within the iS-Graphs framework and evaluated in synthetic and simulated environments. Results show that semantic relations significantly reduce the number of candidate matches, improve computational efficiency, and enable faster convergence, particularly in symmetric scenarios where purely geometric approaches fail.

</details>


### [61] [GS-Playground: A High-Throughput Photorealistic Simulator for Vision-Informed Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25459)
*Yufei Jia,Heng Zhang,Ziheng Zhang,Junzhe Wu,Mingrui Yu,Zifan Wang,Dixuan Jiang,Zheng Li,Chenyu Cao,Zhuoyuan Yu,Xun Yang,Haizhou Ge,Yuchi Zhang,Jiayuan Zhang,Zhenbiao Huang,Tianle Liu,Shenyu Chen,Jiacheng Wang,Bin Xie,Xuran Yao,Xiwa Deng,Guangyu Wang,Jinzhi Zhang,Lei Hao,Zhixing Chen,Yuxiang Chen,Anqi Wang,Hongyun Tian,Yiyi Yan,Zhanxiang Cao,Yizhou Jiang,Hanyang Shao,Yue Li,Lu Shi,Bokui Chen,Wei Sui,Hanqing Cui,Yusen Qin,Ruqi Huang,Lei Han,Tiancai Wang,Guyue Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了GS-Playground，一个多模态仿真框架，通过集成高性能并行物理引擎与批量3D高斯溅射(3DGS)渲染管线，实现了10^4 FPS的高吞吐量视觉强化学习，并引入自动化的Real2Sim工作流来重建真实感环境，有效弥合了具身智能任务中的感知与物理鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能研究正向以视觉为中心的感知范式转变，但现有大规模并行仿真器在视觉任务中的应用受限于以下瓶颈：(1)大规模真实感渲染的计算开销过高；(2)仿真就绪的3D资产创建依赖劳动密集型的手工建模；(3)显著的仿真到现实(sim-to-real)物理差距阻碍了接触丰富的操作策略迁移。

Method: 提出GS-Playground多模态仿真框架，核心创新包括：(1)开发新型高性能并行物理引擎，专门设计与批量3D高斯溅射(3DGS)渲染管线集成，确保高保真同步；(2)引入自动化Real2Sim工作流，重建真实感、物理一致且内存高效的环境，简化复杂仿真场景生成。

Result: 系统在640x480分辨率下实现了10^4 FPS的突破性强吞吐量，显著降低了大规模视觉强化学习的门槛；在 locomotion、navigation 和 manipulation 等多样化具身任务上的大量实验表明，GS-Playground有效弥合了感知差距和物理差距。

Conclusion: GS-Playground通过将高性能并行物理仿真与批量3DGS渲染相结合，并辅以自动化Real2Sim环境重建，为端到端感知学习提供了一个高效、可扩展的仿真平台，推动了视觉驱动的具身智能研究发展。

Abstract: Embodied AI research is undergoing a shift toward vision-centric perceptual paradigms. While massively parallel simulators have catalyzed breakthroughs in proprioception-based locomotion, their potential remains largely untapped for vision-informed tasks due to the prohibitive computational overhead of large-scale photorealistic rendering. Furthermore, the creation of simulation-ready 3D assets heavily relies on labor-intensive manual modeling, while the significant sim-to-real physical gap hinders the transfer of contact-rich manipulation policies. To address these bottlenecks, we propose GS-Playground, a multi-modal simulation framework designed to accelerate end-to-end perceptual learning. We develop a novel high-performance parallel physics engine, specifically designed to integrate with a batch 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) rendering pipeline to ensure high-fidelity synchronization. Our system achieves a breakthrough throughput of 10^4 FPS at 640x480 resolution, significantly lowering the barrier for large-scale visual RL. Additionally, we introduce an automated Real2Sim workflow that reconstructs photorealistic, physically consistent, and memory-efficient environments, streamlining the generation of complex simulation-ready scenes. Extensive experiments on locomotion, navigation, and manipulation demonstrate that GS-Playground effectively bridges the perceptual and physical gaps across diverse embodied tasks. Project homepage: https://gsplayground.github.io.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前尚不清楚如何设计分布式触觉和接近传感器的属性（如感知覆盖范围、类型和范围）以实现机器人的避碰行为。现有研究缺乏对传感器属性与学习型避障行为之间关系的系统性分析。

Method: 提出了一个面向人形机器人H1-2全身避碰的强化学习框架，并以躲避球任务为基准，对机器人上半身分布式传感器的属性进行了消融实验，系统性地研究了传感器覆盖范围、类型和范围等属性对避障行为学习的影响。

Result: 实验发现：(1) 只要感知范围足够，原始接近测量可以替代显式的物体定位；(2) 在样本效率方面，稀疏的非方向性接近信号优于密集的方向性替代方案。

Conclusion: 传感器属性的设计对强化学习避障行为有显著影响，稀疏非方向性接近传感器在样本效率上具有优势，且原始接近数据在足够感知范围下可实现有效避障，为机器人全身传感器配置提供了设计指导。

Abstract: Collision-free motion is often aided by tactile and proximity sensors distributed on the body of the robot due to their resistance to occlusion as opposed to external cameras. However, how to shape the sensor's properties, such as sensing coverage; type; and range, to enable avoidant behavior remains unclear. In this work, we present a reinforcement learning framework for whole-body collision avoidance on a humanoid H1-2 robot and use it to characterize how sensor properties shape learned avoidance behavior. Using dodgeball as a benchmark task, we ablate the properties of sensors distributed across the upper body of the robot and find that raw proximity measurements can substitute for explicit object localization provided the sensing range is sufficient and that sparse non-directional proximity signals outpace dense directional alternatives in sample efficiency.

</details>


### [63] [Improving Sensing Coverage and Compliance of 3D-Printed Artificial Skins Through Multi-Modal Sensing and Soft Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25563)

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合飞行时间与自电容传感的3D打印人工皮肤，集成多模态感知、柔性覆盖层和简化电气接口，在FR3机械臂上实现了接触检测、场景重建和压力触觉响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D打印人工皮肤虽然能够实现全身触觉和接近觉覆盖，但存在单模态传感和刚性材料的局限性，限制了其实际应用价值。为了提升3D打印人工皮肤的实用性和功能性，需要解决多模态集成、柔性覆盖层以及电气接口等关键问题。

Method: 提出了一种混合式飞行时间（ToF）与自电容（SC）传感皮肤。该方法集成了：1）多模态传感融合技术，同时实现接近觉和触觉感知；2）柔性顺应性覆盖层设计，用于冲击吸收和压力传感；3）简化的导电走线与外部电子设备之间的电气接口。通过在FR3机械臂上部署六个包含40个传感单元的人工皮肤单元进行验证。

Result: 实现了接触检测、场景重建以及与顺应性覆盖层压力相关的触觉响应。成功在FR3机械臂上部署了具有40个传感元件的六个人工皮肤单元，验证了多模态传感集成的有效性。

Conclusion: 该混合ToF-SC传感皮肤通过多模态集成、柔性覆盖层和简化电气接口，显著提升了3D打印人工皮肤的实用性和功能性，为机器人全身触觉感知提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: 3D-printed artificial skins are a scalable approach to whole-body tactile and proximity coverage, but prior implementations have been limited to unimodal sensing and rigid materials. To improve the practical usability of 3D-printed artificial skins, we present a hybrid time-of-flight (ToF) and self-capacitance (SC) sensing skin that demonstrates multi-modal sensing integration, soft compliant coverings for impact absorption and pressure sensing, and a streamlined electrical interface between printed conductive traces and external electronics. We show that combining ToF and SC modalities enables contact detection, scene reconstruction, and pressure-correlated tactile responses with the compliant covering by deploying six artificial skin units with 40 sensing elements over an FR3 robot arm.

</details>


### [64] [SlicerRoboTMS: An Open-Source 3D Slicer Extension for Robot-Assisted Transcranial Magnetic Stimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25661)
*Wenzhi Bai,Yituo Guo,Bhaskar Basu,Andrew Weightman,Zhenhong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SlicerRoboTMS 是一个开源 3D Slicer 扩展，为机器人辅助经颅磁刺激（Robo-TMS）研究提供统一平台，支持 MRI 神经导航和机器人系统集成，降低研究门槛并促进可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助经颅磁刺激（Robo-TMS）是一种图像引导的机器人干预技术，可提高传统经颅磁刺激（TMS）的准确性和可重复性。然而，Robo-TMS 的开发具有挑战性，因为它需要医学成像、计算机视觉和机器人学等多学科专业知识。目前缺乏一个统一的开源平台来支持 Robo-TMS 研究，导致入门门槛高、研究难以复现和扩展。

Method: 本文提出了 SlicerRoboTMS，一个基于 3D Slicer 的开源扩展。该扩展利用 3D Slicer 的医学图像计算和可视化功能，支持基于磁共振成像（MRI）的神经导航，并通过标准化通信协议和可配置的系统描述与机器人系统进行接口。该扩展设计为支持多种硬件配置和快速原型开发。

Result: SlicerRoboTMS 提供了一个统一的交互基础设施，可用于 Robo-TMS 研究。通过一个示例集成展示了如何将该扩展纳入代表性的 Robo-TMS 工作流程中。该扩展降低了 Robo-TMS 研究的入门门槛，并促进了可复现和可扩展的研究。

Conclusion: SlicerRoboTMS 是一个开源的 3D Slicer 扩展，为 Robo-TMS 研究提供了统一的平台，支持 MRI 神经导航和机器人系统集成，具有硬件兼容性强、易于快速原型开发的特点，有助于推动该领域的可复现研究。

Abstract: Robot-assisted Transcranial Magnetic Stimulation (Robo-TMS) is an image-guided robotic intervention that enhances the accuracy and reproducibility of conventional Transcranial Magnetic Stimulation (TMS), a widely used non-invasive brain stimulation procedure in clinical treatment and neuroscience research. Despite its potential, the development of Robo-TMS remains challenging due to the need for multidisciplinary expertise spanning medical imaging, computer vision, and robotics. This paper presents SlicerRoboTMS, an open-source 3D Slicer extension that provides a unified interaction infrastructure for Robo-TMS research. By leveraging 3D Slicer's medical image computing and visualisation capabilities, the extension supports Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI)-based neuronavigation and interfaces with robotic systems through standardised communication protocols and configurable system descriptions. An example integration is presented to demonstrate how SlicerRoboTMS can be incorporated into a representative Robo-TMS workflow. Designed to support diverse hardware configurations and rapid prototyping, SlicerRoboTMS lowers the barrier to entry and facilitates reproducible and extensible research in Robo-TMS. The extension is available at https://github.com/OpenRoboTMS/SlicerRoboTMS.

</details>


### [65] [GEGLU-Transformer for IMU-to-EMG Estimation with Few-Shot Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25670)
*Miroljub Mihailovic,Luca Tonin,Stefano Tortora,Emanuele Menegatti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer的IMU-to-EMG学习框架，通过惯性测量单元重建肌肉激活包络，解决了表面肌电图在可穿戴机器人应用中的鲁棒性问题，实现了跨被试泛化和快速个性化适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经肌肉激活的可靠估计是可穿戴机器人自适应个性化控制的关键，但表面肌电图（EMG）由于电极敏感性、信号非平稳性和强被试依赖性，难以在实验室外稳健部署。因此需要一种替代方案来克服这些限制。

Method: 提出了一种自适应IMU-to-EMG学习框架，结合Transformer编码器与高斯误差门控线性单元（GEGLU-Transformer），从可穿戴惯性测量中重建连续肌肉激活包络。该架构通过注意力机制增强跨被试泛化能力，并支持轻量级被试特异性个性化适应（仅需0.5%适应数据）。

Result: 在严格留一被试交叉验证（LOSO）的多条件下肢生物力学数据集上，无被试适应时达到r = 0.706 ± 0.139、R² = 0.474 ± 0.208；使用0.5%适应数据后性能提升至r = 0.761 ± 0.030、R² = 0.559 ± 0.047，表现出快速适应和早期性能饱和特性。

Conclusion: 基于注意力的架构结合轻量级适应是直接EMG传感在真实世界可穿戴机器人应用中的一种实用且可扩展的替代方案，为可穿戴机器人领域的自适应控制提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Reliable estimation of neuromuscular activation is a key enabler for adaptive and personalized control in wearable robotics. However, surface electromyography (EMG) remains difficult to deploy robustly outside laboratory settings due to electrode sensitivity, signal non-stationarity, and strong subject dependence. In this work, we propose an adaptive IMU-to-EMG learning framework that reconstructs continuous muscle activation envelopes from wearable inertial measurements across heterogeneous movement conditions. The approach combines a Transformer encoder with Gaussian Error Gated Linear Units (GEGLU-Transformer) to enhance cross-subject generalization and enable rapid subject-specific personalization. Under a strict leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) protocol on a multi-condition lower-limb biomechanics dataset, the proposed architecture achieves r = 0.706 +/- 0.139 and R^2 = 0.474 +/- 0.208 without subject-specific adaptation. With only 0.5% adaptation data, performance increases to r = 0.761 +/- 0.030 and R^2 = 0.559 +/- 0.047, demonstrating rapid adaptation and early performance saturation. These results support attention-based architectures combined with lightweight adaptation as a practical and scalable alternative to direct EMG sensing for real-world wearable robotic applications.

</details>


### [66] [Learning-Based Dynamics Modeling and Robust Control for Tendon-Driven Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25691)
*Ziqing Zou,Ke Qiu,Fei Wang,Haojian Lu,Rong Xiong,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可微学习框架，将基于GRU的高保真动力学模型与端到端神经控制策略相结合，通过双向多通道连接和残差预测抑制长程自回归误差累积，实现了对腱驱动连续体机器人的精确轨迹跟踪，并在未知负载下表现出优于雅可比方法的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腱驱动连续体机器人（TDCRs）存在摩擦迟滞、传动柔顺性等复杂非线性特性，给建模与控制带来重大挑战；传统方法难以有效补偿这些非线性，且长程预测时误差会不断累积。

Method: 1) 构建基于GRU的动力学模型，采用双向多通道连接和残差预测机制；2) 将该可微模型作为梯度桥梁，通过反向传播端到端优化神经控制策略；3) 使策略隐式内化对复杂非线性的补偿能力。

Result: 在物理三关节TDCR平台上的实验表明：该框架实现了精确的轨迹跟踪，消除了自激振荡，在未知负载干扰下展现出优于基于雅可比方法的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的可微学习框架通过将高保真动力学建模与端到端神经控制深度融合，有效解决了TDCR的复杂非线性控制问题，为连续体机器人的高精度、高鲁棒性控制提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Tendon-Driven Continuum Robots (TDCRs) pose significant modeling and control challenges due to complex nonlinearities, such as frictional hysteresis and transmission compliance. This paper proposes a differentiable learning framework that integrates high-fidelity dynamics modeling with robust neural control. We develop a GRU-based dynamics model featuring bidirectional multi-channel connectivity and residual prediction to effectively suppress compounding errors during long-horizon auto-regressive prediction. By treating this model as a gradient bridge, an end-to-end neural control policy is optimized through backpropagation, allowing it to implicitly internalize compensation for intricate nonlinearities. Experimental validation on a physical three-section TDCR demonstrates that our framework achieves accurate tracking and superior robustness against unseen payloads, outperforming Jacobian-based methods by eliminating self-excited oscillations.

</details>


### [67] [Sensitivity-Based Tube NMPC for Cooperative Aerial Structures Under Parametric Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25766)
*Giuseppe Silano,Quentin Sablé,Marco Tognon,Luigi Iannelli,Antonio Franchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于灵敏度的管状NMPC框架，用于处理具有有界参数不确定性的协作空中链系统。通过在线传播一阶参数状态灵敏度并计算约束收紧裕度，实现了对连杆分离约束和推力约束的鲁棒化，在保持名义NMPC跟踪性能的同时显著改善了不确定性下的约束裕度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）方法在处理具有参数不确定性的协作空中链系统时，缺乏有效的鲁棒性保证。特别是由多飞行器通过刚性连杆连接形成的空中链系统，其连杆质量、长度和转动惯量等参数的不确定性会严重影响控制性能和约束满足。因此，需要开发一种能够在线处理有界参数不确定性的鲁棒NMPC框架，以确保系统在不确定性下的安全性和约束满足。

Method: 本文提出了一种基于灵敏度的管状非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）框架。具体方法包括：(1) 考虑平面双飞行器链系统，通过输入速率驱动建模来强制执行推力和力矩的斜率限制和幅值限制；(2) 利用一阶参数状态灵敏度沿预测时域传播，计算在线约束收紧裕度；(3) 通过光滑余弦嵌入实现连杆间分离约束的鲁棒化；(4) 对推力幅值约束进行鲁棒化处理；(5) 在MATLAB中实现该算法并进行边界贴附机动和蒙特卡洛不确定性采样的评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的方法在不确定性条件下显著改善了约束裕度，同时保持了与名义NMPC相当的跟踪性能。通过边界贴附机动和蒙特卡洛不确定性采样验证了方法的有效性，证明了在参数不确定性存在的情况下，系统仍能满足约束要求并保持良好的控制性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于灵敏度的管状NMPC框架为具有有界参数不确定性的协作空中链系统提供了一种有效的鲁棒控制方案。通过在线传播参数灵敏度并计算约束收紧裕度，该方法能够在保证鲁棒约束满足的同时，不牺牲跟踪性能。该方法为解决多飞行器协作系统中的参数不确定性问题提供了新的思路，具有较好的应用前景。

Abstract: This paper presents a sensitivity-based tube Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC) framework for cooperative aerial chains under bounded parametric uncertainty. We consider a planar two-vehicle chain connected by rigid links, modeled with input-rate actuation to enforce slew-rate and magnitude limits on thrust and torque. Robustness to uncertainty in link mass, length, and inertia is achieved by propagating first-order parametric state sensitivities along the horizon and using them to compute online constraint-tightening margins. We robustify an inter-link separation constraint, implemented via a smooth cosine embedding, and thrust-magnitude bounds. The method is implemented in MATLAB and evaluated with boundary-hugging maneuvers and Monte-Carlo uncertainty sampling. Results show improved constraint margins under uncertainty with tracking performance comparable to nominal NMPC.

</details>


### [68] [KinDER: A Physical Reasoning Benchmark for Robot Learning and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25788)
*Yixuan Huang,Bowen Li,Vaibhav Saxena,Yichao Liang,Utkarsh Aashu Mishra,Liang Ji,Lihan Zha,Jimmy Wu,Nishanth Kumar,Sebastian Scherer,Danfei Xu,Tom Silver*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了KinDER，一个针对机器人运动学和动力学具身推理的基准测试，包含25个程序化生成环境、兼容Gymnasium的Python库、13个基线方法，以及真实到仿真再到真实的实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人系统在物理世界交互时，需要推理运动学和动力学约束，但当前缺乏专门评估物理推理能力的标准化基准，且现有方法在物理推理方面存在显著差距。

Method: 构建KinDER基准测试：1) 设计25个程序化生成环境，隔离五种核心物理推理挑战（基本空间关系、非抓取多物体操作、工具使用、组合几何约束、动态约束）；2) 开发兼容Gymnasium的Python库，包含参数化技能和演示；3) 实现13个基线方法，涵盖任务与运动规划、模仿学习、强化学习和基础模型方法；4) 进行真实到仿真再到真实的实验验证。

Result: 现有方法在KinDER的许多环境中表现困难，表明当前物理推理方法存在实质性差距；同时验证了仿真与真实世界物理交互的对应关系。

Conclusion: KinDER作为完全开源的标准化评估套件，能够系统化比较不同范式，为推进机器人物理推理研究提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Robotic systems that interact with the physical world must reason about kinematic and dynamic constraints imposed by their own embodiment, their environment, and the task at hand. We introduce KinDER, a benchmark for Kinematic and Dynamic Embodied Reasoning that targets physical reasoning challenges arising in robot learning and planning. KinDER comprises 25 procedurally generated environments, a Gymnasium-compatible Python library with parameterized skills and demonstrations, and a standardized evaluation suite with 13 implemented baselines spanning task and motion planning, imitation learning, reinforcement learning, and foundation-model-based approaches. The environments are designed to isolate five core physical reasoning challenges: basic spatial relations, nonprehensile multi-object manipulation, tool use, combinatorial geometric constraints, and dynamic constraints, disentangled from perception, language understanding, and application-specific complexity. Empirical evaluation shows that existing methods struggle to solve many of the environments, indicating substantial gaps in current approaches to physical reasoning. We additionally include real-to-sim-to-real experiments on a mobile manipulator to assess the correspondence between simulation and real-world physical interaction. KinDER is fully open-sourced and intended to enable systematic comparison across diverse paradigms for advancing physical reasoning in robotics. Website and code: https://prpl-group.com/kinder-site/

</details>


### [69] [Privileged Foresight Distillation: Zero-Cost Future Correction for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25859)
*Pengcheng Fang,Hongli Chen,Xiaohao Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出特权预见蒸馏(PFD)方法，将世界动作模型中未来视频分支的作用重新定义为可蒸馏的动作去噪修正，而非简单的正则化或预测目标。通过在推理时仅使用当前帧并添加小型适配器来捕获未来条件修正，在保持当前帧推理速度的同时显著提升了操作任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型在训练时联合预测未来视频和动作，但近期发现未来预测分支在推理时可被移除而性能损失很小，这引发了一个开放问题：未来信息到底扮演什么角色？现有观点认为未来信息仅作为共享视觉骨干网的正则化器，但作者认为这一解释不够充分。

Method: 1. 将特权预见形式化为动作去噪方向上的残差——给定真实未来与仅给定当前帧时模型预测的差异；2. 提出特权预见蒸馏(PFD)，将该残差从训练时教师模型蒸馏到当前帧学生模型的小型适配器中；3. 教师与学生共享相同骨干网，仅在视频token的注意力掩码上存在差异；4. 推理时从不生成未来视频，仅使用当前帧加适配器。

Result: 1. 控制实验验证了性能增益反映的是真实的未来条件修正，而非容量或正则化的副作用；2. 在LIBERO和RoboTwin操作基准上取得一致的性能提升；3. 保持了当前帧-only的推理接口，增加的延迟可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 未来信息在世界动作模型中的作用应被重新框定：不是作为要预测的目标，也不是作为要吸收的正则化器，而是作为可压缩、可蒸馏的修正。联合训练诱导了一种动作条件的修正，而当前帧-only策略仅部分捕获了这种修正。PFD通过蒸馏残差有效地将未来条件知识转移到推理时的高效架构中。

Abstract: World action models jointly predict future video and action during training, raising an open question about what role the future-prediction branch actually plays. A recent finding shows that this branch can be removed at inference with little to no loss on common manipulation benchmarks, suggesting that future information may act merely as a regularizer on the shared visual backbone. We propose instead that joint training induces an action-conditioned correction that privileged future observations impose on action denoising, and that current-only policies capture this correction only partially. Making the account precise, we formulate privileged foresight as a residual in the action-denoising direction -- the difference between what a model predicts given the true future and what it predicts given only the current frame -- and introduce \emph{Privileged Foresight Distillation (PFD)}, which transfers this residual from a training-time teacher into a small adapter on a current-only student. The teacher and student share the same backbone and differ only in the attention mask over video tokens; future video is never generated at inference. Controlled experiments verify that this gain reflects a genuine future-conditioned correction rather than a side effect of capacity or regularization. Empirically, PFD achieves consistent improvements on LIBERO and RoboTwin manipulation benchmarks while preserving the current-only inference interface at negligible added latency. This view reframes the role of future information in world action models: not as a target to predict, nor as a regularizer to absorb, but as a compressible correction to be distilled.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [70] [Co-Director: Agentic Generative Video Storytelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24842)
*Yale Song,Yiwen Song,Nick Losier,Nathan Hodson,Ye Jin,Rhyard Zhu,Yan Xu,Daniel Vlasic,Carina Claassen,Jasmine Leon,Khanh G. LeViet,Zack Chomyn,Joe Timmons,Brett Slatkin,Scott Penberthy,Tomas Pfister*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Co-Director是一种层次化多智能体框架，通过全局优化（多臂老虎机探索方向+局部自精炼保证一致性）解决视频叙事的语义漂移问题，在400场景的GenAD-Bench基准上显著超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前扩散模型虽能生成高保真视频片段，但将其转化为连贯的叙事引擎仍具挑战。现有基于链式模块的智能体流水线通过手工设计提示词自动化视频生成，但存在语义漂移和级联失败问题。

Method: 提出Co-Director，一种层次化多智能体框架，将视频叙事形式化为全局优化问题。核心方法包括：1) 层次化参数化——多臂老虎机全局识别有前景的创作方向；2) 局部多模态自精炼循环——缓解身份漂移并确保序列级一致性。该方法在探索新颖叙事策略与利用有效创作配置之间取得平衡。

Result: 引入GenAD-Bench评估基准，包含400个虚构产品的个性化广告场景数据集。实验表明，Co-Director显著优于最先进的基线方法，且能无缝泛化至更广泛的电影叙事任务。

Conclusion: Co-Director通过全局优化视角和层次化多智能体协作，为视频叙事生成提供了原则性方法，在个性化广告及更广泛的电影叙事场景中展现出优越性能和泛化能力。

Abstract: While diffusion models generate high-fidelity video clips, transforming them into coherent storytelling engines remains challenging. Current agentic pipelines automate this via chained modules but suffer from semantic drift and cascading failures due to independent, handcrafted prompting. We present Co-Director, a hierarchical multi-agent framework formalizing video storytelling as a global optimization problem. To ensure semantic coherence, we introduce hierarchical parameterization: a multi-armed bandit globally identifies promising creative directions, while a local multimodal self-refinement loop mitigates identity drift and ensures sequence-level consistency. This balances the exploration of novel narrative strategies with the exploitation of effective creative configurations. For evaluation, we introduce GenAD-Bench, a 400-scenario dataset of fictional products for personalized advertising. Experiments demonstrate that Co-Director significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, offering a principled approach that seamlessly generalizes to broader cinematic narratives. Project Page: https://co-director-agent.github.io/

</details>


### [71] [Latent Agents: A Post-Training Procedure for Internalized Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24881)
*John Seon Keun Yi,Aaron Mueller,Dokyun Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过两阶段微调将多智能体辩论蒸馏到单个LLM中，实现高达93%的token效率提升；发现内化在激活空间中形成智能体专属子空间，并展示该机制可用于定位和控制有害行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体辩论（multi-agent debate）能提升大语言模型（LLM）的推理能力，但计算开销巨大——需要生成大量长文本对话记录后才能回答问题，效率低下。

Method: 提出一个两阶段微调框架，将多智能体辩论蒸馏到单个LLM中：第一阶段进行辩论结构学习（debate structure learning），第二阶段通过动态奖励调度和长度裁剪实现内化（internalization）。此外，使用激活引导（activation steering）研究内化能力的机制基础。

Result: 在多个模型和基准测试上，内化模型达到或超过显式多智能体辩论性能，同时减少高达93%的token使用量。机制分析发现内化过程在激活空间中形成了对应不同智能体视角的可解释子空间。进一步证明，通过内化辩论将恶意智能体植入LLM后，应用负向引导可有效抑制有害行为，且相比对基础模型进行引导，对整体性能的损害更小。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解蒸馏模型中的多智能体能力提供了新视角，并为控制内化推理行为提供了实用指导，表明蒸馏使有害行为更易于定位和控制。

Abstract: Multi-agent debate has been shown to improve reasoning in large language models (LLMs). However, it is compute-intensive, requiring generation of long transcripts before answering questions. To address this inefficiency, we develop a framework that distills multi-agent debate into a single LLM through a two-stage fine-tuning pipeline combining debate structure learning with internalization via dynamic reward scheduling and length clipping. Across multiple models and benchmarks, our internalized models match or exceed explicit multi-agent debate performance using up to 93% fewer tokens. We then investigate the mechanistic basis of this capability through activation steering, finding that internalization creates agent-specific subspaces: interpretable directions in activation space corresponding to different agent perspectives. We further demonstrate a practical application: by instilling malicious agents into the LLM through internalized debate, then applying negative steering to suppress them, we show that distillation makes harmful behaviors easier to localize and control with smaller reductions in general performance compared to steering base models. Our findings offer a new perspective for understanding multi-agent capabilities in distilled models and provide practical guidelines for controlling internalized reasoning behaviors. Code available at https://github.com/johnsk95/latent_agents

</details>


### [72] [S-SONDO: Self-Supervised Knowledge Distillation for General Audio Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24933)
*Mohammed Ali El Adlouni,Aurian Quelennec,Pierre Chouteau,Geoffroy Peeters,Slim Essid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: S-SONDO 是一种首个仅利用输出嵌入进行通用音频基础模型知识蒸馏的框架，能够将模型压缩至原大小的 1/61，同时保留高达 96% 的教师模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的通用音频基础模型参数量巨大（数亿级别），导致推理成本高且难以在边缘设备部署。现有音频领域的知识蒸馏方法主要依赖有监督设置，需要类别 logits、中间特征或特定架构技术，无法适用于仅输出嵌入的自监督或度量学习模型。

Method: 提出 S-SONDO（Self-Supervised KnOwledge DistillatioN for General AuDio FOundation Models）框架，仅使用教师模型的输出嵌入进行知识蒸馏，无需 logits 或层级别对齐，具有架构无关性。方法包含基于聚类的平衡数据采样策略，并研究了损失函数的选择。

Result: 将两个音频基础模型蒸馏为三个高效学生模型，学生模型最大可缩小至教师模型的 1/61，同时保留高达 96% 的教师模型性能。

Conclusion: S-SONDO 是首个仅利用嵌入进行通用音频基础模型蒸馏的框架，证明了无需 logits 或中间特征即可实现高效模型压缩，为边缘设备部署大规模音频模型提供了可行方案。

Abstract: General audio foundation models have recently achieved remarkable progress, enabling strong performance across diverse tasks. However, state-of-the-art models remain extremely large, often with hundreds of millions of parameters, leading to high inference costs and limited deployability on edge devices. Knowledge distillation is a proven strategy for model compression, but prior work in audio has mostly focused on supervised settings, relying on class logits, intermediate features, or architecture-specific techniques. Such assumptions exclude models that output only embeddings, such as self-supervised or metric-learning models. We introduce S-SONDO (Self-Supervised KnOwledge DistillatioN for General AuDio FOundation Models), the first framework to distill general audio models using only their output embeddings. By avoiding the need for logits or layer-level alignment, S-SONDO is architecture-agnostic and broadly applicable to embedding-based teachers. We demonstrate its effectiveness by distilling two audio foundation models into three efficient students that are up to 61 times smaller while retaining up to 96% of teacher performance. We also provide practical insights on loss choice and clustering-based balanced data sampling. Code is available here: https://github.com/MedAliAdlouni/ssondo.

</details>


### [73] [Adaptive Prompt Embedding Optimization for LLM Jailbreaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24983)
*Miles Q. Li,Benjamin C. M. Fung,Boyang Li,Radin Hamidi Rad,Ebrahim Bagheri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PEO是一种直接优化原始提示嵌入的白盒越狱攻击，无需附加对抗词元即可保持提示语义，并在有害行为基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的白盒越狱攻击通常通过在用户提示后附加离散的对抗性后缀来实现，这会明显改变提示内容，并在组合词元空间中操作。此前研究避免直接优化原始提示词元的嵌入，因为担心扰动会破坏提示的语义内容。本文旨在验证这一担忧是否成立，并提出一种更隐蔽、更有效的攻击方法。

Method: 提出提示嵌入优化（PEO），一种多轮白盒越狱攻击方法。该方法直接优化原始提示词元的嵌入，不附加任何对抗性词元。PEO结合连续嵌入空间优化、结构化续写目标以及自适应的失败聚焦调度策略。在后续轮次中，使用启发式复合响应支架（非自然独立模板）来增强攻击效果。

Result: 实验表明，优化后的嵌入与其原始嵌入足够接近，在最近词元投影后可见提示字符串被完全保留。定量分析显示模型响应在绝大多数提示下保持主题相关性。在两个标准有害行为基准测试中，PEO超越了所有竞争方法，包括离散后缀搜索、附加对抗性嵌入和基于搜索的对抗生成等白盒攻击。

Conclusion: 对原始提示嵌入进行直接优化的担忧是没有根据的。PEO能够在保持提示语义和外观不变的前提下实现高效越狱，且其性能增益并非空洞的格式操作或仅支架输出。该研究揭示了嵌入空间优化的潜力，并对LLM对齐安全性提出了新的挑战。

Abstract: Existing white-box jailbreak attacks against aligned LLMs typically append discrete adversarial suffixes to the user prompt, which visibly alters the prompt and operates in a combinatorial token space. Prior work has avoided directly optimizing the embeddings of the original prompt tokens, presumably because perturbing them risks destroying the prompt's semantic content. We propose Prompt Embedding Optimization (PEO), a multi-round white-box jailbreak that directly optimizes the embeddings of the original prompt tokens without appending any adversarial tokens, and show that the concern is unfounded: the optimized embeddings remain close enough to their originals that the visible prompt string is preserved exactly after nearest-token projection, and quantitative analysis shows the model's responses stay on topic for the large majority of prompts. PEO combines continuous embedding-space optimization with structured continuation targets and an adaptive failure-focused schedule. Counterintuitively, later PEO rounds can benefit from heuristic composite response scaffolds that are not natural standalone templates, yet ASR-Judge shows that the resulting gains are not merely empty formatting or scaffold-only outputs. Across two standard harmful-behavior benchmarks and competing white-box attacks spanning discrete suffix search, appended adversarial embeddings, and search-based adversarial generation, PEO outperforms all of them in our experiments.

</details>


### [74] [Assessing Y-Axis Influence: Bias in Multimodal Language Models on Chart-to-Table Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24987)
